Admin V2 Stage E (Module 10 - Access Control & Platform Teams Checkpoint - 22 October 2026):
- Completed Module 10 (Access Control & Platform Teams at `/admin/access`):
  1. Strict Platform Staff vs Tenant Separation: All platform delegation operates strictly on `platform_admins` and `platform_admin_scopes`, completely separated from customer companies and branch staff (`company_memberships`).
  2. Scoped Platform Role Catalog: Established 4 distinct internal roles with least-privilege capability boundaries:
     - `owner` (Super Admin): Sole sovereign owner (`velorahelperai@gmail.com`).
     - `support` (Support Admin): Account search, tickets, reviews, channel inspection (no financial/billing or AI modifications).
     - `finance` (Finance Admin): Billing ledger, payments, plan catalog (no AI, tenant data, or admin management).
     - `operations` (Operations Admin): Observability center, correlation logs, AI router telemetry, webhook health (no financial data).
  3. Full Delegation Lifecycle & Instant Revocation: Implemented DAL in `src/lib/tenancy/access-actions.ts` and `src/lib/tenancy/access-guards.ts`:
     - `adminGetPlatformAccessOverview`: lists owner and delegated staff with active status.
     - `adminGrantPlatformAdminRole`: assigns scoped role and capabilities, strictly rejects escalation to owner (`FORBIDDEN`).
     - `adminUpdatePlatformAdminRole`: updates role/status/notes, protects owner from modification.
     - `adminToggleAdminStatus`: suspends/activates admins; suspended status immediately rejects open sessions at database check (`requirePlatformRole`).
     - `adminRevokePlatformAdmin`: deletes admin role and scopes, protected against owner deletion.
     - All mutations audited immutably to `admin_audit_logs`.
  4. Light Petrol Enterprise UI (`src/components/admin/admin-platform-access.tsx`): 4 KPI cards (Total Staff, Active Delegated Admins, Anti-Escalation LOCKED, Immediate Session Invalidation), Role Catalog guide, staff directory with status badges and action buttons, plus interactive Grant, Edit, and Revoke confirmation modals.
  5. Next.js 15 Server Page (`src/app/admin/access/page.tsx`) with `AccessGate` fallback.

## Current onboarding decision replacement — 2026-10-19, 19:49 UTC

Authority: revised specification Sentinel_2a5162463d348191b6c800ddb7126bd5 (19:48 UTC), explicitly confirmed as a decision by Sentinel_1d40f7cdfec48191afe89eb9fc132753 (19:49 UTC). Goals and channels do not determine the badge. The customer may freely choose another persona. Do not recommend Standard/basic versus Premium, change prices, grant entitlements or imply the recommended persona can accommodate the team.
- Capacity remains per purchased branch: Standard/basic includes the owner plus 2 employees and 1 channel; Premium includes the owner plus 4 employees and 2 channels. The owner is excluded from the employee allowance. Show this capacity truth separately from the persona badge; a Dana badge for a team larger than 10 is not a capacity promise. Subscription prices, currency, term and immutable branch rights remain unchanged.
- Website learning quota is approved at 10 pages for basic and 50 pages for Premium. This replaces the deferred 20/100 proposal. Website input remains optional and learning begins after payment. Page counting, scope, enforcement and import-cost implementation remain pending; the approved numbers are not pending. These learning limits are distinct from the existing Velora website creation addon of up to 5 pages.
- Campaign prepaid balance is denominated in USD, replacing the earlier OMR proposal. It remains separate from subscription charges and AI reply credits. Show campaign cost before launch. Campaign pricing, refunds, currency conversion and provider integration remain later-stage work. Subscription currency is unchanged.
- Preserve the approved sequence: local business data and questions → plan → review → payment → create company and branches → connect channels and assistant learning. No free service trial before payment. Answers stay in current-visit memory before payment and are saved to company data only after verified payment, with isolation and permissions; that storage decision remains approved despite the revised pasted specification calling it open. The minimal schema/provisioning implementation remains pending.
- Image preview approval is still required before the three-screen UI implementation. Social-link ban claims and +968 coexistence eligibility remain unverified; do not assert them or introduce a Meta-mandated domain block.

### ES02 concurrent provisioning gap - closed (2026-10-19)

15/15 additional real PostgreSQL assertions PASS; the previous111unit/128Chrome receipts are retained unchanged and were not rerun. Two distinct PostgreSQL backends invoked the current provisioning RPC for the same existing approved synthetic account/session, verified synthetic settlement/order, and operation UUID. The first transaction was held after successful provision; the second actual RPC was observed waiting on the first backend's native advisory authority lock before the first COMMIT. After commit the second returned reuse with the identical company/order/order-group receipt. Actual committed result:1company,4branches,1order,1verified settlement,1provisioning,1billing subscription,1activation foundation,1credit balance,4branch rights. Phones and distinct authoritative branch IDs match; business-wide contact stays unchanged.

Retry contract checked: a valid changed phone payload reuses the completed immutable receipt and cannot overwrite contacts/IDs/effects; malformed later-branch phone returns INVALID_INPUT before reuse. The existing contract does not promise rejection of every valid changed completed retry payload. No product or migration change was required.

Isolation: a disposable logical snapshot of the existing owned fixture was used because separate concurrent backends must commit to see each other's result; a single outer rollback cannot encompass both. Existing identities/session/lineage and current RPCs were copied, with no new account or credential. Unrelated pg_cron/cron scheduler was excluded because it is restricted to the canonical database name. Existing native supabase_admin was used only to restore original owners; both tested RPC backends remained authenticated through the actual registered session checks. PostgreSQL acldefault expands NULL owner ACL to its equivalent exact grantor/options set when comparing restored metadata; no grants, RLS, role attributes or authority functions were changed. Source before/after security is additionally compared byte-for-byte with the original accepted security baseline.

## WP01A A7 redirect-origin regression isolated and fixed — 2026-10-17
Isolated real Next smoke reproduced GET http://127.0.0.1:3320/auth/callback returning HTTP307 Location origin http://localhost:3320. route.ts used new URL(request.url).origin, whose Next internal URL differed from browser origin. Browser cookies are host-scoped and protocol runner deliberately permits only exact app/gateway origins; redirected navigation can complete while subsequent foreign-origin JS assets are blocked, explaining missing English locale and SSR-only Arabic loading fence. No justification to weaken AccountSessionProvider, cookie/intent binding or PKCE.
New route-origin.test.ts reproduced two failing assertions before source fix (evidence wp01a-origin-regression-before.log); both pass after minimal finish() normalization of same-origin redirects to relative pathname+query+fragment. Browser A7 now also explicitly asserts final origin remains original app origin before checking /reset-password.
